CORPORATE PROFILE
公司概況
China Foods Limited (“China Foods” or the “Company” and together with its
subsidiaries, the “Group”), a subsidiary of COFCO Corporation (“COFCO”) and the
only focused beverage platform of COFCO, is listed on the main board of The Stock
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (Stock Code: 506).


The Company mainly manufactures, distributes, markets and sells the Coca-Cola
series products at nineteen provincial-level administrative regions in the PRC through
COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited (“COFCO Coca-Cola”), a 65%-owned joint-venture
with The Coca-Cola Company. COFCO Coca-Cola offers twenty-four brands of products
in ten major types of beverages which include sparkling drink, juice, water, milk
drink, energy drink, tea, coffee, functional nutrition drink, sports drink and plant-
based protein drink.


中國食品有限公司（簡稱「中國食品」或「本公司」
                      ，連同其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）為中
糧集團有限公司（「中糧」）的附屬公司及中糧唯一的專業化飲料業務平台，並於香港聯
合交易所有限公司主板上市（股份代號：506）
                     。


本公司目前主要通過與可口可樂公司合作成立並持有65%權益的中糧可口可樂飲料有
限公司（簡稱「中糧可口可樂」）在國內19個省級行政區域從事生產、配送、推廣和銷售
可口可樂系列產品，向消費者提供包括汽水、果汁、水、乳飲料、能量飲料、茶、咖啡、
功能型營養素飲料、運動飲料及植物蛋白飲料等10大品類、24個品牌的產品。

2   CHINA FOODS LIMITED ANNUAL REPORT 2022
                                                                              MAJOR EVENTS IN 2022
                                                                                    2022年大事記



 Full commitment for the beverage item availability and on-site services in Beijing Winter Olympics
 全力支持北京冬季奧運會的保供及現場服務




In 2022, China successfully hosted the 24th edition of the Olympic Winter Games. To ensure the smooth progress
of the Games and to provide better service for the sport competitions, referees, workforce and spectators, COFCO
Coca-Cola’s 53 employees, coming from 17 bottling plants, joined the Coca-Cola Winter Olympics volunteer team,
which was allocated in three different competition areas among Beijing and Zhangjiakou and served 12 competition
venues, 24 non-competition venues, three athletes’ villages and three media centers. In addition, the Company
provided 1,280 refrigerators and 192 Costa coffee machines, so as to ensure beverage supply during the
competitions.
2022年，中國成功舉辦第24屆奧林匹克冬季運動會。為保證運動會順利進行，為運動會、裁判員、工作人員以及
觀眾更好服務，中糧可口可樂17個裝瓶廠53名員工參加了可口可樂冬奧志願團隊，分佈在北京、張家口的三個賽
事區，服務12個競賽場館、24個非競賽場館、3個運動員村和3個媒體中心。同時，公司提供1,280台冰箱及192台
Costa現磨咖啡機，保障賽事期間飲料供應。




                                                                                      中 國 食 品 有 限 公 司 年報 2022      3
    MAJOR EVENTS IN 2022 2 0 2 2 年 大 事 記




     Promoting digital transformation in the Company comprehensively
     全面推動公司數字化轉型




    In 2022, COFCO Coca-Cola began its digital transformation comprehensively. We have established a digital
    governance committee and office, for making reasonable assessment of long-term objectives and short-term needs,
    thus integrating digital transformation strategy with our business needs and enhancement. Through the transformation
    from the old “scattered point” to the strategic “system-based” of information system building, we have broadened
    our digital engagement from exploring digital applications to establishing a digital new norm. The construction of
    data platform, data governance system and digital team capabilities was already in progress, which is aiming to
    lay a solid foundation for realizing the blueprint for digitization and big-data application.
    2022年中糧可口可樂全面啟動數字化轉型。成立數字化治理委員會和辦公室，合理評估長期目標和短期需求，將
    數字化轉型規劃落地和業務需求完善有機結合，系統建設從過往的「散點式」向戰略引領的「系統式」提升，從開發
    數字化應用向構建數字化生態過渡。數據中台、數據治理體系和數字化團隊能力的建設已經啟動，為數字化藍圖
    的實現和大數據的應用打下堅實基礎。




4   CHINA FOODS LIMITED ANNUAL REPORT 2022
                                                                               MAJOR EVENTS IN 2022 2 0 2 2 年 大 事 記




 “Rubik’s Cube” project on market refinement launched
 市場精細化管理的魔方項目正式啟動




In 2022, the “Rubik’s Cube” (魔方) project of COFCO Coca-Cola was launched among 20 bottling plants. The
“Rubik’s Cube” project combined the source of internal and external data, for the purpose of subdividing the
market scientifically, seeking reasonable benchmarking targets, providing quantitative opportunity analysis, assisting
the team in respect of decision-making with science base and providing accurate implementation. The project
facilitated COFCO Coca-Cola in pushing forward its digital transformation and high-quality development.
2022年中糧可口可樂魔方項目在20家裝瓶廠啟動應用。魔方項目通過將內外部數據結合，將市場科學細分，尋找
合理對標對象，提供量化機會分析，幫助團隊科學決策、精準執行，助力中糧可口可樂數字化轉型和高質量發展。




                                                                                          中 國 食 品 有 限 公 司 年報 2022        5
    MAJOR EVENTS IN 2022 2 0 2 2 年 大 事 記




     The establishment of Yuexiang Club, which is wholly owned by China Foods and focused on providing online
     services for consumers
     中國食品全資悅享會公司正式成立，聚焦全場景消費者線上服務




    On 13 June 2022, COFCO Yuexiang Club (Hainan) Digital Technology Co., Ltd. (中糧悅享會（海南）數字科技有
    限公司) (“Yuexiang Club”) was established. Yuexiang Club is 100% controlled by China Foods. The purpose of
    which is to adapt the changes in consumer trends, improve corporate competitiveness and build-up the channel
    to reach the consumers directly. Through the construction of operation system for consumers, we are capable to
    analyze consumer demand, realize accurate marketing strategies for targeted customers, as well as enhance online
    sales. In the future, Yuexiang Club will facilitate the Company in strengthening our communication with customers,
    promoting high-quality development with more accurate products and marketing strategies.
    2022年6月13日，中糧悅享會（海南）數字科技有限公司（「悅享會」）正式創建成立。悅享會由中國食品100%控股，
    成立目的是為了適應消費趨勢變化，提升企業競爭力，搭建直接觸達消費者的能力，通過構建消費者運營體系，分
    析消費者需求，實現精準營銷賦能客戶的同時也提升線上渠道的銷售。未來悅享會將助力公司強化消費者溝通，
    以更加精準的產品和營銷促進高質量發展。




6   CHINA FOODS LIMITED ANNUAL REPORT 2022
                                                                           MAJOR EVENTS IN 2022 2 0 2 2 年 大 事 記




 The 20th bottling plant, COFCO Coca-Cola Guizhou Plant, was completed in construction and commenced its
 production and operation
 第二十家裝瓶廠－中糧可口可樂貴州廠建設完畢並投入生產運營




The 20th bottling plant of COFCO Coca-Cola, Guizhou Plant, started its operation on 29 April 2022, and commenced
its production on 10 July 2022. With the commencement of production of the Guizhou Plant, the supply chain
layout of COFCO Coca-Cola in the southwestern part of China was further improved, resulting in a linkage effect
between Sichuan, Chongqing and Guizhou provinces and cities, and greatly improving the efficiency of the supply
chain. At the same time, it will accurately connect with the consumption demand of the region, so as to achieve
more efficient production on demand, and continue to provide consumers in the southwest with a wide range of
quality and diversified beverage options.
中糧可口可樂第二十家裝瓶廠                      貴州廠於2022年4月29日啟動生產，7月10日正式投產。貴州廠的投產，進一步
完善了中糧可口可樂在西南區域的供應鏈佈局，形成川渝黔三省市聯動效應，大幅提升供應鏈效率。同時將精準
對接本地區的消費需求，實現更高效的按需生產，繼續為西南地區消費者提供優質多元化的飲品選擇。




                                                                                     中 國 食 品 有 限 公 司 年報 2022       7
    MAJOR EVENTS IN 2022 2 0 2 2 年 大 事 記




     COFCO Coca-Cola has awarded the Coca-Cola Global Best Commercial Leadership Awards for the fifth
     consecutive year, and the 2021 Coca-Cola Greater China and Mongolia “Candler Cup” champion! Got the Coca-
     Cola Global “Candler Cup” final again!
     中糧可口可樂連續第五年獲得可口可樂系統全球商務領導力大獎，並蟬聯2021年可口可樂大中華及蒙古區「坎
     德勒」杯冠軍！再次入圍可口可樂全球「坎德勒」杯決賽！




    COFCO Coca-Cola retained its champion in the 2022 Coca-Cola Greater China and Mongolia “Candler Cup”
    competition on the back of its strong performance, reaching the final of the Coca-Cola Global “Candler Cup”.
    在2022年可口可樂大中華及蒙古區「坎德勒」杯爭奪賽中，中糧可口可樂憑藉優秀的業績表現蟬聯桂冠，再次入圍
    可口可樂全球「坎德勒」杯決賽。

    In the Coca-Cola Global Best Commercial Leadership Contest, with our advanced technology, good consumer
    experience and the notable results in practical application of the COFCO Coca-Cola “Thermoelectric Cooler and
    Warmer” (冷暖雙溫櫃) project, we have awarded the Coca-Cola Global Best Commercial Leadership Awards for
    the fifth consecutive year.
    在可口可樂系統全球商務領導力大賽中，中糧可口可樂「冷暖雙溫櫃」項目以其先進的技術、良好的消費者體驗以
    及實際應用的顯著成果，連續第五次摘取年度可口可樂系統「全球客戶及商務領導力大獎」                                              。




8   CHINA FOODS LIMITED ANNUAL REPORT 2022
                                                                             MAJOR EVENTS IN 2022 2 0 2 2 年 大 事 記




 Completion of the acquisition of the non-sparkling production lines and promoting the development of our
 beverage business
 完成非汽水生產線的收購工作，推動全品類飲料業務發展




In 2022, through almost one year of planning, communication and negotiation, COFCO Coca-Cola completed the
acquisition of non-sparkling production lines among its operation area. The project filled up the deficiency in the
production of non-sparkling beverages (i.e. fruit juice, fruit milk, tea and coffee) by COFCO Coca-Cola, and marked
a new stage of development for COFCO Coca-Cola. It is of great significance our enterprise to further optimize
its product structure, respond to market demand and promote its strategy on industrial capacity.
2022年，通過近一年的規劃、溝通、談判，中糧可口可樂完成了對經營區域內非汽水生產線的收購工作。這一項目
填補了中糧可口可樂在果汁、果奶、茶飲料、咖啡等非汽水產品生產上的空白，標誌著中糧可口可樂進入了一個新
的發展階段，對企業進一步優化品類結構、響應市場需求、推動產業產能佈局有著重要的意義。

MR. QING LIJUN
Mr. Qing, 53, was appointed the chairman of the board of the Company in September 2022 and has been as
an executive director and the managing director of the Company since January 2020. Mr. Qing is currently a
director and the chief executive officer of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited, a 65%-owned subsidiary of the
Company, and a director of certain subsidiaries of the Company. Mr. Qing joined COFCO Corporation and/or its
subsidiaries in July 1993 and has held various positions including the director of strategy department of COFCO
Corporation during the period from July 2016 to March 2019. Mr. Qing was the deputy general manager of COFCO
Coca-Cola Beverages Limited during the period from December 2007 to June 2016. Mr. Qing has concurrently
served as the strategy planning director of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited during the period April 2004 to
March 2014. During the period from April 2004 to June 2016, Mr. Qing has also concurrently served various
positions including the director of the public relations department and the director of the finance department of
COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited, the general manager of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages (Shandong) Limited
and the general manager of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages (Beijing) Limited. Mr. Qing has served as the deputy
general manager of Jilin COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited during the period from June 2002 to March 2004
and a sales operation manager of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited during April 2000 to May 2002.

Mr. Qing graduated from Renmin University of China and Guanghua School of Business Management of Peking
University with a Bachelor’s degree in Economics and a Master’s degree in Business Administration, respectively.
Mr. Qing has extensive experience in beverage production, marketing, strategy planning, finance, public relations
and general management.

                                                      INDEPENDENT NON-EXECUTIVE DIRECTOR
                                                      獨立非執行董事
                                                      MS. LEUNG KA LAI, ADA, SBS     梁家麗女士（銀紫荊星章）



MS. LEUNG KA LAI, ADA, SBS
Ms. Leung, age 62, was appointed as an independent non-executive director of the Company in June 2022. Ms.
Leung is currently a member of Standing Commission on Civil Service Salaries and Conditions of Service of the
HKSAR Government. She joined the HKSAR Government in September 1983 and has served in the civil service
for 35 years until her retirement in 2019. Ms. Leung joined the Intellectual Property Department (IPD) of the
HKSAR Government as a senior solicitor in 1995 and was the director of Intellectual Property and Registrar of
Trade Marks, Patents and Designs since May 2014 until her retirement in March 2019. Prior to joining IPD, Ms.
Leung was a senior crown counsel with the then Attorney General’s Chambers (now Department of Justice). She
had worked with both the Litigation Unit and the Advisory Unit in the Civil Division. Ms. Leung was also a part-
time Lecturer, Faculty of Law, of University of Hong Kong during the period from October 2020 to August 2021.
Ms. Leung was appointed as an Official Justice of Peace by the HKSAR Government since 2012 and until February
2020. She was awarded the Silver Bauhinia Star (SBS) by the HKSAR Government in 2018.
Ms. Leung has over 20 years professional experience in IP law practice as well as financial and staff management,
management of outsourcing contracts as well as planning and implementing promotion and public education
programmes in the public sector. She also has extensive experience in formulation of policy, law and practice in
different areas of intellectual property as well as building and maintaining network with local and international
stakeholders, including the legal profession, intellectual property practitioners, trade and business organisations,
overseas and Mainland counterparts and inter-governmental organisations. She has also represented Hong Kong,
China in different international conferences and meetings in formulating IP policies at the international level. She
also has experience in civil advisory, civil litigation and criminal prosecution with the then Attorney General’s
Chambers (now Department of Justice).
Ms. Leung holds a Bachelor Degree in Science from the University of Hong Kong. She then obtained a Government
Legal Training Scholarship and studied law in the University of Buckingham in England. She was called to the
Bar in England and in Hong Kong.

梁家麗女士（銀紫荊星章）
梁女士，62歲，於2022年6月獲委任為本公司獨立非執行董事。梁女士現為香港特別行政區政府公務員薪俸及服
務條件常務委員會委員。她於1983年9月加入香港特別行政區政府，在公務員隊伍中服務了35年，直至2019年退
休。梁女士於1995年加入香港特別行政區政府知識產權署擔任高級律師，自2014年5月起任知識產權署署長，以
及商標、專利和外觀設計註冊處處長，至2019年3月退休。在加入知識產權署之前，梁女士為當時的律政署（現為
律政司）的高級律師。她曾在民事部門的訴訟組和法律諮詢組工作。梁女士亦於2020年10月至2021年8月期間擔
任香港大學法律系兼任講師。
梁女士自2012年起至2020年2月獲香港特別行政區政府委任為官方太平紳士。她於2018年獲香港特別行政區政府
頒授銀紫荊星章。
梁女士在知識產權法律實踐、財務和員工管理、外包合同管理以及規劃和實施公共部門的宣傳和公共教育計劃方
面擁有超過20年的專業經驗。她在制定知識產權不同領域的政策、法律和實踐方面也擁有豐富的經驗，以及與本
地和國際持份者建立和維持聯繫，包括法律專業、知識產權從業人員、貿易和商業組織、海外和內地同行和政府間
組織。她曾代表中國香港參加不同的國際會議，在國際層面制定知識產權政策。她還具有於當時的律政署（現為律
政司）從事民事意見、民事訴訟和刑事檢控方面的經驗。
梁女士持有香港大學理學士學位。隨後，她獲得了政府法律培訓獎學金，並在英國白金漢大學學習法律。她在英國
和香港獲大律師資格。

            Relevant social responsibility issues that cover corporate governance, environmental protection,
            interests and rights of employees, supply chain management, customer responsibility and
            community investment were identified by collating feedbacks from daily communication with
            stakeholders, and collecting major issues related to the Company's operation and management, with             Issues Identification
            reference to the international and domestic sustainable development guidelines and industry
            practices based on the ESG Reporting Guide of the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited                              議題識別

            報告指引》的基礎上參考國際國內可持續發展指引及同業實踐，識別出覆蓋公司治理、環境保護、員工

            stakeholders of Company were invited to evaluate the materiality of each issue from their own                  Issues Evaluation
            perspective

            程度

3.   GREEN DEVELOPMENT                                                 3.   綠色發展
     Nowadays, climate and environmental issues have become                 如 今，氣 候 及 環 境 問 題 已 成 為 全 球
     a global challenge. Enterprises play a key role in tackling            共 同 面 臨 的 難 題，企 業 是 應 對 氣 候
     climate change and protecting the Earth. As a leading                  變化，保護地球家園不可或缺的主力
     company in China’s beverage industry, China Foods has                  軍，中國食品作為中國飲料行業領先
     always put into practice the development philosophy that               企業，始終貫徹「綠水青山就是金山
     “lucid waters and lush mountains are invaluable assets”,               銀山」的發展理念，堅持「節約能源資
     upheld the basic principles of “saving energy resources,               源，減少污染排放，積極保護環境、
     reducing pollutant emissions, actively protecting the                  履行社會責任」的基本原則，在商業
     environment and fulfilling social responsibility”, actively            活動及公司日常運營中，積極實踐環
     implemented environmental protection measures in                       保措施，在有效利用水資源、提升能
     commercial activities and routine operations, and established          源效率和包裝物減量上，建立起長效
     a long-term management mechanism from the aspects of                   管理機制，實現可持續發展。
     effective utilization of water resources, improvement of energy
     efficiency and reduction of packaging, so as to achieve
     sustainable development.

     Special topic: Strive to achieve low-carbon transformation             專題：竭力實現低碳轉型，應對氣候
     and tackle climate change                                              變化
     In recent years, global climate risks have been increasing,            近年來全球氣候風險不斷上升，氣候
     and climate change has become a severe challenge facing                變化是全人類共同面臨的嚴峻挑戰。
     all human beings. In this context, China Foods has actively            中國食品積極落實國家政策，高度重
     implemented national policies, attached great importance to            視生產運營過程中溫室氣體排放，主
     greenhouse gas emissions during production and operation,              動參照氣候相關財務信息披露工作組
     and promoted the integration of climate change response                (TCFD)建議，圍繞氣候變化帶來的實
     into corporate governance, risk management system and                  體風險和轉型風險，推動應對氣候變
     routine operations, with focus on the physical and transition          化融入公司治理、風險管理體系和日
     risks brought by climate change, according to the suggestions          常運營，以技術創新引領發展方向，
     of the Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures             持續開展綠色健康飲品等產品研發，
     (TCFD). Led by technological innovation, China Foods has               有效提升環保能力水平，為守護綠水
     continuously developed products such as green and healthy              青山增磚添瓦，以實際行動為應對氣
     drinks, improved its environmental protection capabilities,            候變化作出貢獻。
     and contributed to protecting lucid waters and lush mountains
     and tackling climate change through practical actions.

               Case: We Care about green and low carbon – COFCO Coca-Cola Sichuan Plant became a “Low Carbon + Action”
               response entity
               案例：綠色低碳我們在乎－中糧可口可樂四川廠成為低碳+行動響應企業

               In November 2022, China Foods Sichuan Plant became a “Low Carbon + Action” response entity
               and won the honorary title of “Fashion Brand” at the press conference of “Tan Hui Tian Fu Low
               Carbon+ Action” sponsored by Chengdu Ecological Environment Bureau and Chengdu Commerce
               Bureau.
               2022年11月，中國食品四川廠在成都市生態環境局、市商務局主辦的「碳惠天府之低碳+行動」發佈會
               上分別成為和榮獲「低碳+行動」響應企業、                   「風尚品牌」榮譽稱號。

               Chengdu is the first city in China that proposes a carbon benefit mechanism, which combines the
               concepts of “credit rewards for public carbon emission reduction” and “development and operation
               of project carbon emission reduction”. As one of the first leading enterprises joining the mechanism,
               COFCO Coca-Cola Sichuan Plant fully advocated green and low-carbon behaviors, and guided the
               public to live a low-carbon life, which has helped to attract more than 700,000 users for the “Tan
               Hui Tian Fu” platform.
               成都市在國內首創提出構建以「公眾碳減排積分獎勵、項目碳減排量開發運營」為雙路徑的碳普惠機
               制。中糧可口可樂四川廠作為首批龍頭企業加入聯動，全面倡導綠色低碳行為，引導公眾踐行低碳生
               活，已助力「碳惠天府」平台吸引超70萬用戶參與。

              Case: Recycling and reuse of waste cans
              案例:廢品易拉罐回收處理和再利用

              The Company performs fine management of the waste cans from production lines, which are
              uniformly delivered to and briquetted by the cans supplier and then sold to the aluminum
              factory for treatment. The downstream waste products are returned to the upstream aluminum
              factory by taking advantage of the supplier’s resources, thus realizing the recycling of waste
              aluminum cans. In the future, we will further explore and promote the relegation and utilization
              of waste cans.
              公司將生產線上的廢品易拉罐進行精細化管理，統一回收給易拉罐供應商，由易拉罐供應商進
              行壓塊，並賣給鋁材廠處理。利用供應商的資源優勢，將下游廢品返還給我們的上游鋁材廠，實
              現了廢品鋁罐的再利用。未來，我們將繼續在廢品易拉罐的保級利用方面做更多的探索和推進。




                                             Fine Management of Waste Cans
                                                  廢品易拉罐精細化管理

          Company wholly abides by national, regional and industry                 質產品。本公司嚴格遵守包括《中華

          of China on Protection of Consumer Rights and Interests,                 合規指引》等相關的國家、地區及行
          the Market Business Compliance Guideline , the Sales                     業法律法規和國際質量管理標準，並
          Business Compliance Guideline and the Internet                           制定公司內部管理制度，切實做到把

          6.1   Control of Product Quality                                         6.1   把控產品質量
                China Foods maintains a corporate culture of quality                     中國食品持續推行公司的質量
                and safety, and further enhances its product quality                     文 化，繼 續 提 升 本 公 司 食 品 質
                and safety management. The Company’s quality and                         量 和 食 品 安 全 管 理 水 平。本 公

                at factory level, as reviewed by a third-party professional              的 質 量 安 全 體 系 認 證，並 由 第
                certification organization. As of the end of 2022, all                   三方專業認證機構指派經驗豐
                subordinate production enterprises of the Company                        富 的 審 核 專 家 負 責 審 核。截 至
                had obtained FSSC 22000 and ISO 9001 certifications.                     2022年底，本公司所屬正式投

                                                                                         ISO9001體系認證。

                Taking into consideration the risks inherent to its                      本公司充分考慮上下游和製造
                upstream, downstream and manufacturing phases,                           環 節 的 風 險，在 重 視 傳 統 意 義
                while emphasizing the construction of the HACCP                          製 造 環 節HACCP管 理 體 系 建
                management system for traditional manufacturing                          設 的 基 礎 上，向 前 延 伸 至 供 應
                process, the Company also extended its focus toward                      商、新產品開發，向後延伸至通
                its suppliers and new product development, and                           路客戶服務系統。
                backward to the customer service system.

                  Rural Revitalization (continued)                                  （續）

                  Case: Charming Huyi Embraces “Ganjile” – COFCO Coca-Cola Brand Country Fair Unveiled
                  案例：大美鄠邑      「趕集樂」    －中糧可口可樂品牌鄉集鳴鑼開市

                 “Ganjile” brand country fair is one of the series activities of rural revitalization carried out
                 by COFCO Coca-Cola Shaanxi Plant, which is in the theme of “empowering children of the
                 earth and revitalizing the beautiful countryside”. It is also a practice of carrying forward the
                 achievements of Youth 100 “Countryside Classroom” project and seeking contributions to rural
                 revitalization in cooperation with rural entrepreneurs. COFCO Coca-Cola explored the value of
                 rural areas from diversified aspects through Ganjile brand country fair. With rural landscape as
                 a catalyst, and following the core concept with such key words as “ecology, empowerment of
                 peasants, and sustainability”, COFCO Coca-Cola unfolded the beauty and promoted goods of the
                 rural area, to showcase the achievements of rural entrepreneurs, and maximize the value of the
                 beautiful countryside, and meanwhile introduced popular culture and special delicacies to the
                 daily life of villagers, so as to create economic and social circular value for the locality.
                 「趕集樂」品牌鄉集是中糧可口可樂陝西廠「賦能大地之子、振興大美鄉村」為主題的系列鄉村振興活
                 動之一，也是延續青年100「鄉間課堂」項目成果與鄉創客們探索企業助力鄉村振興的實踐。中糧可口
                 可樂通過趕集樂－品牌鄉集活動挖掘農村多元價值空間，以鄉村田園景觀為助燃劑，以「生態、賦農、
                 可持續」為核心理念，打開大美鄉村之美，推介鄉村好物，展示鄉創客成果，最大限度發揮美麗鄉村的
                 價值，也讓流行文化、特色美食等走進村民的日常生活，為當地創造經濟與社會循環價值。

                 Donation to pandemic prevention
                 防疫捐助

                 In support for the normalized prevention and control of the COVID-19 pandemic, COFCO Coca-
                 Cola actively participated in the prevention and control of the pandemic in the 7 provinces,
                 autonomous regions or municipalities covered by its business in various forms, including public
                 welfare volunteering activities, epidemic prevention materials and drinking water donations,
                 and donated a total of 142,000 bottles of drinking water and RMB50,000 to the front line of
                 epidemic prevention in various regions, helping front-line epidemic prevention workers to ensure
                 their drinking water safety with the strength of its own industry.
                 在新冠肺炎疫情常態化下，中糧可口可樂通過公益志願、防疫物資及飲用水捐贈等多種形式，積極參
                 與到業務覆蓋的7個省市區疫情防控中，累計向各地防疫一線捐贈飲用水142,000餘瓶，捐贈現金5萬
                 元，以自身產業所長，助力一線防疫工作者，保障他們的飲水安全。

           Disaster assistance
           災害援助

           COFCO Coca-Cola owns 20 bottling plants covering 81% of the territorial area of China and
           49.4% of its population. In cases of natural disaster, the plants’ strong marketing network and
           logistical resources are harnessed to bring essentials such as drinking water to where it is vitally
           needed. In 2022, the Group responded to the “Clean Water in 24 Hours” rescue mechanism on
           15 occasions, providing more than 650,000 bottles of water to victims and rescuers in disaster-
           stricken areas.
           中糧可口可樂擁有20家裝瓶廠，覆蓋了81%的國土面積和49.4%的中國大陸人口。基於強大的營銷網
           絡和物流資源，在同胞有難之時，及時將飲用水運抵重災區，幫助災民緩解無水困境。2022年，本集
           團共響應「淨水24小時」救援機制15次，第一時間為災區同胞和救援人員支援65萬餘瓶飲用水及飲料。

                 In response to calls for a “Carbon Peaking and Carbon Neutrality”, as well as Coca-Cola’s own
                 vision of a “World Without Waste”, COFCO Coca-Cola carried out public welfare environmental
                 protection projects such as waste classification education, bottle recycling activities and RPET
                 recycling derivatives exhibitions in its operating areas, contributing to the ecological well-being
                 with practical actions. In 2022, the Group carried out 85 “Double Carbon” environmental
                 protection activities.
                 中糧可口可樂積極響應「碳達峰碳中和」號召，結合可口可樂「天下無廢」願景，在經營區域開展垃圾
                 分類教育、舊瓶回收環保活動、RPET回收衍生品展等公益環保項目，以實際行動助力打贏生態文明攻
                 堅。2022年，本集團在各經營區域開展「雙碳」環保項目活動85場。

                                               本公司開展環保主題公益宣傳活動

maintaining high standards of corporate governance to safeguarding   企業管治，以保障股東、投資者、客戶及員
the interests of our shareholders, investors, customers and staff,   工之利益及提升其企業價值及問責能力。本
and enhance its corporate value and accountability. Directors of     公司董事（「董事」）明白將良好的企業管治
the Company (the “Director(s)”) recognize the importance of          元素納入本集團之管理架構、內部控制及風

management structure, internal control and risk management           機制。

      THE BOARD OF DIRECTORS                                                   董事會
      Responsibilities of the Board                                            董事會的責任

of interest will be present at such meetings.

      Risk Management and Internal Control                                    風險管理及內部監控
      The Board acknowledges its responsibility for maintaining               董事會確認其對維持適合及有效的風險管
      appropriate and effective risk management and internal control          理和內部監控制度承擔責任，並通過董事會
      systems, and for reviewing the effectiveness of these systems           審核委員會檢討這些系統的有效性。
      through the audit committee of the Board.

      Sound risk management and internal control systems are based            健全的風險管理和內部監控體系是基於本
      on the Company’s good business ethics and culture, the quality          公司良好的商業道德與文化、員工的素質與
      and capabilities of its employees, the direction of the Board, and      能力、董事會的方向以及管理層的效率與能
      also the efficiency and capabilities of management.                     力。

      Management of the Company, under the Board supervision, is              本公司的管理層在董事會的監督下，負責風
      responsible for the design, implementation and monitoring of the        險管理和內部監控系統的設計，實施和監
      risk management and internal control systems.                           督。

      The Group has established a sound and comprehensive risk                本集團已經建立起完善的全面風險管理和
      management and internal control system, formed a systematic             內部監控體系，形成了科學有效的職責分工
      and effective system on segregation of duty and three lines of          體系，建立了風險管理三道防線，即業務部
      defense in risk management, with each of the business units as          門為第一道防線；各級業務監督檢查部門
      the first line of defense; the business supervision and inspection      為第二道防線；內審部和監察部為第三道
      departments at all levels as the second line of defense; and internal   防線。
      audit department and supervision department as the third line of
      defense.

      The Group has established its risk management and internal control      本 集 團 依 照COSO (Committee of Sponsoring
      systems based on the COSO (Committee of Sponsoring Organisation         Organisations of the Treadway Commission)
      of the Treadway Commission) risk management framework.                  的風險管理框架搭建風險管理及內部監控
                                                                              系統。

      The Group’s risk management covers the Company’s headquarters           本集團的風險管理工作覆蓋本公司總部及
      and its subsidiaries, covering various risks faced in operation and     各下屬公司，內容涵蓋經營管理中面臨的各
      management, and focusing on and continuously monitor the key            種風險，並對其中的關鍵風險進行重點關注
      risks.                                                                  和持續監督。

      According to the risk management strategy, the Group conducts           根據風險管理策略，結合本集團的戰略和經
      a comprehensive risk assessment every two years in combination          營目標，以及市場環境的變化，本集團每兩
      with the Group’s strategic and business objectives and changes          年實施一次全面風險評估。識別對實現戰略
      in the market environment. Identify major risks that adversely          目標造成不利影響的主要風險，並根據一套
      affect the achievement of strategic objectives and prioritise the       標準準則評估及排列所識別風險的優先次
      identified risks based on a set of standard criteria and formulate      序，制定風險緩解計劃以便將風險監控至可
      a risk mitigation plan to monitor the risks to an acceptable level.     接受水準。

      The Group conducted a comprehensive risk assessment in 2021.            本集團於2021年進行了一次全面風險評估
      The internal audit department summarised and sorted the                 工作，內審部根據評估結果進行了匯總和
      assessment results, and selected the top ten risks with the highest     排序，取評分最高的前十大風險為本集團
      scores as the main risks faced by the Group in 2021-2022, and           2021-2022年面對的主要風險並制定了相
      formulated corresponding monitoring measures. These risks mainly        應的監控措施。該等風險主要涵蓋市場競
      cover market competition, safe production, cost pressure, China-        爭、安全生產、成本壓力、中美關係和人才
      United States relations, and talent supply.                             供給等方面。

The Group has consistently dealt with and monitored the major              本集團對評估出的重大風險進行持續的應
risks that were assessed and identified the responsible departments        對和監督，明確責任部門和責任人，管理流
and accountable persons, the management process and the                    程及相應報告機制。
corresponding reporting mechanism.

All bottling plants and departments had implemented risk                   各裝瓶廠和各部門認真積極實施風險應對

      to enable the preparation of consolidated financial statements that     陳述所需的內部控制負責。
      are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error.

      In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors       在擬備綜合財務報表時，董事負責評估貴集
      are responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a      團持續經營的能力，並在適用情況下披露與
      going concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going      持續經營有關的事項，以及使用持續經營為
      concern and using the going concern basis of accounting unless          會計基礎，除非董事有意將貴集團清盤或停
      the directors either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease          止經營，或別無其他實際的替代方案。
      operations, or have no realistic alternative but to do so.

      The Audit Committee is responsible for overseeing the Group’s           審核委員會負責監督貴集團的財務報告過
      financial reporting process.                                            程。

3   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                   3   主要會計政策
    3.1 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                 3.1   合併及權益會計原則
         i.    Subsidiaries                                               i.    附屬公司
               Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured              附屬公司指本集團對其擁有控
               entities) over which the Group has control. The                  制權的所有實體（包括結構性實
               Group controls an entity where the Group is                      體）。當本集團因參與該實體而
               exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns                   承擔可變回報之風險或享有可
               from its involvement with the entity and has the                 變 回 報 之 權 利，並 有 能 力 透 過
               ability to affect those returns through its power                其指揮該實體活動之權力影響
               to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries             該 等 回 報，則 本 集 團 控 制 該 實
               are fully consolidated from the date on which                    體。附 屬 公 司 自 控 制 權 轉 移 至
               control is transferred to the Group. They are                    本集團當日起全面綜合入賬。
               deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                於控制權終止當日起停止合併
                                                                                入賬。

               Inter-company transactions, balances and                         集 團 內 公 司 間 之 交 易、結 餘 及
               unrealised gains on transactions between group                   未 變 現 收 益 均 予 以 對 銷。未 變
               companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are                  現 虧 損 亦 予 以 對 銷，除 非 交 易
               also eliminated unless the transaction provides                  有證據顯示所轉讓資產出現減
               evidence of an impairment of the transferred                     值。附 屬 公 司 的 會 計 政 策 已 在
               asset. Accounting policies of subsidiaries have                  有 需 要 時 作 出 改 變，以 確 保 與
               been changed where necessary to ensure                           本集團所採納的政策一致。
               consistency with the policies adopted by the
               Group.

               Non-controlling interests in the results and                     非控股權益於附屬公司業績及
               equity of subsidiaries are shown separately in                   權 益 中 分 別 於 綜 合 損 益 表、全
               the consolidated statement of profit or loss,                    面 收 益 表、權 益 變 動 表 及 財 務
               statement of comprehensive income, statement                     狀況表內呈列。
               of changes in equity and statement of financial
               position respectively.

               has significant influence but not control or joint               影 響 力，但 無 控 制 權 或 共 同 控
               control. This is generally the case where the                    制 權 之 所 有 實 體。本 集 團 一 般
               Group holds between 20% and 50% of the                           持 有20%至50%表 決 權。於 聯
               voting rights. Investments in associates are                     營公司之投資於初步按成本確
               accounted for using the equity method of                         認 後 採 用 權 益 會 計 法（ 見 下 文

                       Under the equity method of accounting, the                            根據權益會計法，投資初

                       share of movements in other comprehensive                             資方的其他全面收益變

                       income. Dividends received or receivable from                         股息乃確認為投資賬面
                       associates are recognised as a reduction in the                       值之減少。
                       carrying amount of the investment.

                       does not recognise further losses, unless it has                      應收款項），則 本 集 團 不

                       of the other entity.                                                  本集團已代其他實體承
                                                                                             擔責任或作出付款。

                       Unrealised gains on transactions between the                          本集團與其聯營公司之

                account for all business combinations, regardless of                  資 產，所 有 業 務 合 併 均 採 用 收
                whether equity instruments or other assets are                        購 會 計 法 入 賬。就 收 購 附 屬 公
                acquired. The consideration transferred for the                       司事項而轉讓之代價包括：
                acquisition of a subsidiary comprises the:

                •     fair values of the assets transferred                           •   所轉讓資產之公平值

                      acquired business                                                   生之負債

                      a contingent consideration arrangement, and                         何資產或負債之公平值，
                                                                                          以及

                •     fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in               •   該附屬公司中任何原有
                      the subsidiary.                                                     的股權之公平值。

                Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and                      在業務合併中收購的可識別資
                contingent liabilities assumed in a business combination              產以及所承擔的負債及或然負
                are, with limited exceptions, measured initially at their             債，除少數例外情況外，初步按
                fair values at the acquisition date. The group recognises             其於收購事項日期的公平值計
                any non-controlling interest in the acquired entity on                量。本 集 團 按 逐 項 收 購 事 項 基
                an acquisition-by-acquisition basis either at fair value              準以公平值或按非控股權益應
                or at the non-controlling interest’s proportionate share              佔收購實體可識別資產淨值之
                of the acquired entity’s net identifiable assets.                     比例確認於收購實體之任何非
                                                                                      控股權益。

                Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                   收購事項相關成本於產生時支
                                                                                      銷。

                The excess of the consideration transferred, the                      所 轉 讓 代 價、於 收 購 實 體 之 任
                amount of any non-controlling interest in the acquired                何非控股權益金額及任何先前
                entity and the acquisition-date fair value of any                     於收購實體之股權於收購事項
                previous equity interest in the acquired entity over the              日期之公平值超出所收購可識
                fair value of the net identifiable assets acquired is                 別資產淨值之公平值入賬為商
                recorded as goodwill. If those amounts are less than                  譽。倘 該 等 金 額 低 於 所 收 購 業
                the fair value of the net identifiable assets of the                  務可識別資產淨值之公平值，
                business acquired, the difference is recognised directly              則差額將直接於損益確認為議
                in profit or loss as a bargain purchase.                              價收購。

         and receivable.

         comprehensive income of the subsidiary in the period                  立財務報表之賬面值超過被投

         not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised                 行 減 值 測 試。減 值 虧 損 按 資 產

         generating units). Non-financial assets other than                   現金流入。除商譽外，已出現減

         reporting period.

                probable. They are measured at the lower of their                     等資產乃按其賬面值及公平值
                carrying amount and fair value less costs to sell, except             減 銷 售 成 本 之 較 低 者 計 量，惟
                for assets such as deferred income tax assets, assets                 本要求明確豁免的遞延所得稅

                contractual rights under insurance contracts, which                   物業及保險合約下之合約權利
                are specifically exempt from this requirement.                        等資產則除外。

                An impairment loss is recognised for any initial or                   倘資產（或出售組別）於任何初

                to fair value less costs to sell. A gain is recognised                成本，則確認減值虧損。倘其後
                for any subsequent increases in fair value less costs                 任何增加之公允值減出售資產
                to sell of an asset (or disposal group), but not in                   （或出售組別）之成本，則確認
                excess of any cumulative impairment loss previously                   收 益，惟 不 得 超 出 先 前 已 確 認
                recognised. A gain or loss not previously recognised                  之 任 何 累 計 減 值 虧 損。先 前 並
                by the date of the sale of the non-current asset (or                  未於出售非流動資產（或出售組
                disposal group) is recognised at the date of                          別）日期確認之收益或虧損於終
                derecognition.                                                        止確認日期確認。

                Non-current assets (including those that are part of                  非流動資產（包括屬出售組別之
                a disposal group) are not depreciated or amortised                    資產）於分類為持有待售時不予
                while they are classified as held for sale. Interest and              折 舊 或 攤 銷。分 類 為 持 有 待 售
                other expenses attributable to the liabilities of a                   之出售組別負債應佔之利息及
                disposal group classified as held for sale continue to                其他開支持續確認。
                be recognised.

                the effective interest method. Fees paid on the                        之費用於部分或全部融資將很

                down. In this case, the fee is deferred until the draw-                跡象顯示該貸款將很可能部分
                down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence that                   或 全 部 提 取 之 情 況 下，該 費 用
                it is probable that some or all of the facility will be                資本化為流動資金服務之預付
                drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a prepayment                     款項，並於有關融資期間攤銷。
                for liquidity services and amortised over the period of
                the facility to which it relates.

                Borrowings are removed from the statement of financial                當 合 約 所 訂 明 責 任 獲 解 除、註
                position when the obligation specified in the contract                銷 或 屆 滿 時，借 貸 自 財 務 狀 況
                is discharged, cancelled or expired. The difference                   表 中 剔 除。已 註 銷 或 轉 讓 予 另
                between the carrying amount of a financial liability                  一方之金融負債之賬面值與已
                that has been extinguished or transferred to another                  付代價（包括任何已轉讓非現金
                party and the consideration paid, including any non-                  資產或所承擔負債）間之差額乃
                cash assets transferred or liabilities assumed, is                    於損益確認為融資成本。
                recognised in profit or loss as finance costs.

                Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless               除非本集團有權無條件將負債
                the group has an unconditional right to defer settlement              的結算遞延至報告期後至少
                of the liability for at least 12 months after the reporting           十 二 個 月，否 則 借 貸 分 類 為 流
                period.                                                               動負債。

                Borrowing costs are expensed in the period in which                   借貸成本於產生期間支銷。
                they are incurred.

    3.17 Current and deferred income tax                                3.17 即期及遞延所得稅
         The income tax expense or credit for the period is                  期內所得稅支出或抵免為本期
         the tax payable on the current period’s taxable income              應課稅收入項根據各司法轄區
         based on the applicable income tax rate for each                    適用之所得稅率計算之應繳稅
         jurisdiction, adjusted by changes in deferred income                項，並 經 暫 時 差 額 應 佔 遞 延 所
         tax assets and liabilities attributable to temporary                得稅資產及負債變動及未動用
         differences and to unused tax losses.                               稅項虧損調整。

         Current income tax                                                 即期所得稅
         The current income tax charge is calculated on the                 即期所得稅支出乃根據本公司
         basis of the tax laws enacted or substantively enacted             及其附屬公司及聯營公司經營
         at the end of the reporting period in the countries                及產生應課稅收入之國家於報
         where the company and its subsidiaries and associates              告期末已頒布或實質頒布之稅
         operate and generate taxable income. Management                    法 計 算。管 理 層 就 適 用 稅 務 法
         periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns              例詮釋所規限之情況定期評
         with respect to situations in which applicable tax                 估 報 稅 表 之 狀 況，並 考 慮 稅 務
         regulation is subject to interpretation and considers              機關是否可能接受不確定之稅
         whether it is probable that a taxation authority will              務 處 理。本 集 團 根 據 最 可 能 之
         accept an uncertain tax treatment. The Group                       金額或預期價值計量其稅項結
         measures its tax balances either based on the most                 餘，視 乎 哪 種 方 法 能 為 解 決 不
         likely amount or the expected value, depending on                  確定性提供更好的預測。
         which method provides a better prediction of the
         resolution of the uncertainty.

         Deferred income tax                                                遞延所得稅
         Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the                 遞延所得稅採用負債法就資產
         liability method, on temporary differences arising                 及負債之稅基與其於綜合財務
         between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and                報表之賬面值間之暫時差額作
         their carrying amounts in the consolidated financial               出全數撥備。然而，倘遞延所得
         statements. However, deferred income tax liabilities               稅負債來自商譽之初步確認，
         are not recognised if they arise from the initial                  則 不 予 確 認。倘 遞 延 所 得 稅 來
         recognition of goodwill. Deferred income tax is also               自在交易（業務合併除外）中對
         not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition            資 產 或 負 債 的 初 步 確 認，而 在
         of an asset or liability in a transaction other than a             交易時不影響會計損益或應課
         business combination that at the time of the transaction           稅 損 益，且 並 無 產 生 等 額 應 課
         affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss              稅 及 可 扣 稅 暫 時 差 額，則 不 會
         and does not give rise to equal taxable and deductible             入 賬。遞 延 所 得 稅 採 用 於 報 告
         temporary differences. Deferred income tax is                      期 末 前 已 頒 佈 或 實 質 頒 佈，並
         determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been               預期於變現相關遞延所得稅資
         enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the                 產或償還遞延所得稅負債時適
         reporting period and are expected to apply when the                用之稅率（及法例）釐定。
         related deferred income tax asset is realised or the
         deferred income tax liability is settled.

                The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a                         於（或就此）完成履約義務時，
                performance obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control”            本 集 團 確 認 收 入，即 於 特 定 履
                of the goods or services underlying the particular                  約義務的相關商品或服務的
                performance obligation is transferred to the customer.             「控制權」轉讓予客戶時確認。

                A performance obligation represents a good or service             履約義務指不同的商品及服務
                (or a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or            （或一組商品或服務）或一系列
                a series of distinct goods or services that are                   大致相等的不同商品及服務。
                substantially the same.

                Control is transferred over time and revenue is                    控 制 權 隨 時 間 轉 移，而 倘 滿 足
                recognised over time by reference to the progress                  以 下 其 中 一 項 標 準，則 收 入 乃
                towards complete satisfaction of the relevant                      參照完全滿足相關履約義務的
                performance obligation if one of the following criteria            進展情況而隨時間確認：
                is met:

                •     the customer simultaneously receives and                     •    於本集團履約時，客戶同
                      consumes the benefits provided by the Group’s                     時取得並耗用本集團履
                      performance as the Group performs;                                約所提供的利益；

                •     the Group’s performance creates or enhances                  •    本集團之履約產生或強
                      an asset that the customer controls as the Group                  化一項資產，該資產於本
                      performs; or                                                      集團履約時乃由客戶控
                                                                                        制；或

                •     the Group’s performance does not create an                   •    本集團的履約並未產生
                      asset with an alternative use to the Group and                    對本集團有替代用途的
                      the Group has an enforceable right to payment                     資產，且本集團對迄今已
                      for performance completed to date.                                完成履約之付款具有可
                                                                                        執行之權利。

                Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time                否 則，收 入 於 客 戶 獲 得 商 品 或
                when the customer obtains control of the distinct goods            服務控制權的時間點確認。
                or service.

    3.21 Leases                                                           3.21 租賃
         Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                   租賃於租賃資產可供本集團使
         corresponding liability at the date at which the leased               用當日確認為使用權資產及相
         asset is available for use by the Group.                              應負債。

         Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease                        合約可包含租賃及非租賃部
         components. The Group allocates the consideration                     分。本 集 團 將 合 約 中 之 代 價 按
         in the contract to the lease and non-lease components                 租賃及非租賃部份之相對獨立
         based on their relative stand-alone prices. However,                  價 格 分 配。然 而，就 本 集 團 為
         for leases of real estate for which the Group is a                    承 租 人 之 房 地 產 租 賃 而 言，其
         lessee, it has elected not to separate lease and non-                 選擇不將租賃及非租賃部分分
         lease components and instead accounts for these as                    開，而 將 該 等 部 分 入 賬 為 單 一
         a single lease component.                                             租賃部分。

         Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and                 租賃條款乃按個別基準磋商，
         contain a wide range of different terms and conditions.               並包含廣泛的不同條款及條
         The lease agreements do not impose any covenants                      件。除 出 租 人 持 有 之 租 賃 資 產
         other than the security interests in the leased assets                之 擔 保 權 益 外，租 賃 協 定 並 無
         that are held by the lessor. Leased assets may not be                 附 加 任 何 契 諾。租 賃 資 產 不 得
         used as security for borrowing purposes.                              用作借款的擔保。

         Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially             來自租賃之資產及負債初步按
         measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities                  現 值 基 準 計 量。租 賃 負 債 包 括
         include the net present value of the following lease                  下列租賃付款之淨現值：
         payments:

         •      fixed payments (including in-substance fixed                        •    固 定 付 款（ 包 括 實
                payments), less any lease incentives receivable;                         質上是固定之付
                                                                                         款 ）減 任 何 應 收 租
                                                                                         賃優惠；

         •      variable lease payments that are based on an                        •    取決於生效之日初
                index or a rate, initially measured using the index                      始計量所使用的指
                or rate as at the commencement date.                                     數或比率之可變租
                                                                                         賃付款。

                The lease payments are discounted using the interest                  租賃付款按租賃所隱含之利
                rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily            率 折 現。倘 該 利 率 無 法 輕 易 確
                determined, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate                   定，則 使 用 承 租 人 的 增 量 借 貸
                is used, being the rate that the individual lessee would              利率，即在條件、擔保和條件相
                have to pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain                   近 的 相 似 經 濟 環 境 中，個 人 承
                an asset of similar value to the right-of-use asset in                租人必須支付借款以獲得與使
                a similar economic environment with similar terms,                    用權資產具有類似價值的資產
                security and conditions.                                              所需的利率。

                To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the                     為 釐 定 借 貸 利 率 之 遞 增，本 集
                Group:                                                               團：

                 •    uses recent third-party financing received by the              •    以個人承租人最近獲得
                      individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to                  的第三方融資為起點，調
                      reflect changes in financing conditions since                       整以反映自收到第三方
                      third party financing was received.                                 融資以來融資條件的變
                                                                                          化。

                Lease payments are allocated between principal and                   租賃付款在本金及融資成本之
                finance cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or               間 分 配。融 資 成 本 於 租 賃 期 內
                loss over the lease period so as to produce a constant               自 損 益 扣 除，以 就 各 期 間 之 負
                periodic rate of interest on the remaining balance of                債結餘得出固定定期利率。
                the liability for each period.

                Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising                  使 用 權 資 產 按 成 本 計 量，成 本
                the following:                                                       包括下列各項：

                      liability;                                                          額；

                      received;                                                           已收租賃優惠；

                Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the               使用權資產一般按資產可用年
                shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease term                限或租期兩者之較短者以直線
                on a straight-line basis.                                            法折舊。

    4.2 Capital management                                             4.2   資本管理
         The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities               本 集 團 管 理 其 資 本，以 確 保 本
         in the Group will be able to continue as a going                    集 團 的 實 體 能 夠 持 續 經 營，同
         concern while maximising the return to stakeholders                 時透過優化債務及股權平衡最
         through the optimisation of the debt and equity                     大 限 度 地 提 高 股 東 回 報。本 集
         balance. The Group’s overall strategy remains                       團整體策略與上年保持不變。
         unchanged from prior year.

         The capital structure of the Group consists of net of               本集團之資本架構包括扣除現
         cash and cash equivalents and equity attributable to                金及現金等值項目以及本公司
         owners of the Company, comprising issued share                      擁有人應佔權益（包括已發行股
         capital, share premium and reserves.                                本、股份溢價及儲備）
                                                                                      。

         The Directors review the capital structure on regular               董 事 定 期 審 閱 資 本 架 構。作 為
         basis. As part of this review, the Directors consider               該 審 閱 的 一 部 分，管 理 層 將 考
         the cost of capital and the risks associated with each              慮資本成本及與各類別資本相
         class of capital. Based on recommendations of the                   關的風險。根據董事的建議，本

                 expenditure has not yet been undertaken are included in                 未承辦支出計入綜合財務狀況表中

                 relating to these grants.

           No provision for taxation in Hong Kong has been made as         由於本集團的收入並非於香港產生或
           the Group’s income neither arises in, nor is derived from,      取得，故並無就香港稅項計提撥備。
           Hong Kong.

           The provision for PRC current income tax is based on a         中國即期所得稅撥備乃根據本集團按
           statutory income tax rate of 25% of the assessable income      照中國相關所得稅法規及規例釐定的
           of the Group as determined in accordance with the relevant     應課稅的法定所得稅稅率25%計算，
           income tax rules and regulations of the PRC, except for        惟 於2022年 本 集 團 的 若 干 中 國 實 體

           Impairment testing of goodwill and franchise rights                   商譽及使用壽命不確定之特許經營權
           with indefinite useful lives (continued)                              減值測試（續）
           The Group has determined the values assigned to each of               本集團釐定上述各項主要假設之價值
           the above key assumptions as follows:                                 如下：


            Assumption                     Approach used to determining values
            假設                             用於確定價值之方法
            Sales revenue                 Average annual growth rate over the five-year forecast period; based on past
                                            performance and management’s expectations of market development.
            銷售收入                          五年預測期間的平均年增長率；及管理層對市場發展之預期而釐定。
            Budgeted gross margin         Based on past performance and management’s expectations for the future.
            預測的毛利率                        基於過往表現及管理層對未來之預期。
            Long-term revenue growth rate This is the weighted average growth rate used to extrapolate cash flows
                                            beyond the budget period. The rates are consistent with forecasts included
                                            in industry reports.
            長期收入增長率                       此乃用以推斷預算期間以外現金流量之加權平均增長率。該比率與行業報
                                            告所載預測一致。
            Pre-tax discount rate         Reflect specific risks relating to the relevant segments and the countries in
                                            which the Group operates.
            稅前折現率                         反映有關分部及本集團經營所在國家之特定風險。

          (the “CREs”). Pursuant to the approvals received by                        10%之 預 扣 稅。根 據 本 公 司 獲
          the Company from the State Administration of Taxation                      中國國家稅務局頒發的批文，
          of the PRC, the Company and most of its offshore                           本公司及其大部份境外註冊成
          incorporated subsidiaries are regarded as CREs and                         立的附屬公司被認定為中國居
          relevant enterprise income tax policies shall be                           民 企 業，及 自2013年1月1日 起
          applicable to the Company and these subsidiaries                           適用有關企業所得稅法。因此，
          starting from 1 January 2013. As a result, the Company                     本公司及此等附屬公司將無須
          and these subsidiaries are therefore no longer liable                      就其於中國內地成立之附屬公
          for withholding taxes on dividends distributed by their                    司產生的保留盈利分派的股息

   (d)   Transactions and balances with other State-owned                (d)   與其他國有企業的交易及結餘
         Enterprises
         The Group operates in an economic environment                         本集團運營的經濟環境中的企
         predominated by enterprises directly or indirectly                    業大多為中國政府通過其眾多
         owned or controlled by the PRC government through                     機 關、聯 屬 機 構 或 其 他 組 織 直
         its numerous authorities, affiliates or other organisations           接或間接擁有或控制的企業（統
         (collectively “State-owned Enterprises”). During the                  稱「國有企業」）    。本年度本集團
         year, the Group entered into extensive transactions                   與某些國有企業進行的廣泛交
         covering, but not limited to, sales of goods, purchases               易 包 括 但 不 限 於 銷 售 產 品、購
         of raw materials, property, plant and equipment and                   買 原 材 料、物 業、廠 房 及 設 備
         other assets, and receiving of services with certain                  及 其 他 資 產，並 在 中 糧 集 團 有
         State-owned Enterprises, other than COFCO Corporation                 限公司及其聯屬公司以外的國
         and its affiliates, on terms comparable to those with                 有 企 業 接 受 服 務，此 等 交 易 的
         non-State-owned Enterprises. The Directors consider                   條款與非國有企業交易條款相
         that transactions with those State-owned Enterprises                  似。董事認為，與該等國有企業
         were entered into in the ordinary and usual course of                 的交易為在本集團日常業務過
         business of the Group, and that dealings of the Group                 程 中 進 行 的 活 動，且 本 集 團 與
         have not been significantly or unduly affected by the                 該等中國政府最終控制或擁有
         fact that the Group and those State-owned Enterprises                 的國有企業的交易並沒有受到
         are ultimately controlled or owned by the PRC                         重 大 或 不 當 影 響。本 集 團 還 制
         government. The Group has also established pricing                    定 產 品 和 服 務 的 定 價 政 策，有
         policies for products and services, and such policies                 關政策並不取決於客戶是否為
